# Current calendar, independent of diagnostic refresh.
sed_live_month_key = { value = var:sed_calendar_month_key }
# Country scope. Fixed expenditure is gross fixed income minus its net balance.
sed_fixed_expenses_value = { value = fixed_income subtract = net_fixed_income min = 0 }
sed_live_build_budget_gold_gap = {
 value = var:sed_build_budget_required_gold subtract = gold_reserves min = 0
}
# Readiness adds bounded tie-breaking weight to eligible local projects.
sed_site_readiness = {
 value = sed_state_labor_available divide = 10000 max = 10
 add = { value = infrastructure_delta min = 0 max = 10 }
 add = { value = market_access min = 0 max = 1 multiply = 10 }
}
# State scope; demographic proxy rather than a worker count from the engine.
sed_state_labor_pool = {
 value = state_population min = 0
 multiply = { value = 0.25 add = modifier:state_working_adult_ratio_add min = 0.05 max = 1 }
 multiply = {
  value = state_unemployment_rate min = 0 max = 1
  add = { value = "pop_type_percent_state(pop_type:peasants)" min = 0 max = 1 multiply = 0.5 }
  max = 1
 }
}

sed_state_labor_available = {
 value = sed_state_labor_pool subtract = sed_state_labor_committed min = 0
}
sed_input_railway_coal = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_railway = { has_active_production_method = pm_early_trains } } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_railway = { has_active_production_method = pm_steam_trains } } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_railway = { has_active_production_method = pm_steam_trains_principle_transport_3 } } add = 6 }
 min = 0
}
sed_founding_input_railway_coal = {
 value = 0
 add = { value = 0
 min = { value = 0 if = { limit = { owner = {  } } add = 2 } }
 min = { value = 0 if = { limit = { owner = { has_technology_researched = steel_railway_cars } } add = 5 } }
 min = { value = 0 if = { limit = { owner = { has_technology_researched = steel_railway_cars } } add = 6 } }
 }
 add = { value = 0
 }
}

 value = 0
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_railway }
  add = { value = sed_input_railway_coal
   multiply = { value = b:building_railway.level_after_queued_constructions
    subtract = { value = b:building_railway.level multiply = { value = b:building_railway.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }
    min = 0
   }
   multiply = 1.25
  }

sed_input_country_headroom_coal = {
 value = 0
 market ?= { mg:coal ?= { add = { value = market_goods_sell_orders min = 0 multiply = 2 subtract = { value = market_goods_buy_orders min = 0 } } } }
 subtract = { value = 0 every_scope_state = { limit = { } add = sed_input_pending_coal } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = var:sed_calendar_month_key
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_coal
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_coal }
}
sed_input_headroom_coal = { value = owner.var:sed_input_market_margin_coal }

sed_input_country_headroom_oil = {
 value = 0
 market ?= { mg:oil ?= { add = { value = market_goods_sell_orders min = 0 multiply = 2 subtract = { value = market_goods_buy_orders min = 0 } } } }
 subtract = { value = 0 every_scope_state = { limit = { } add = sed_input_pending_oil } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = var:sed_calendar_month_key
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_oil
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_oil }
}

sed_investment_market_price_wood_lower = {
 value = 15
 market ?= { mg:wood ?= {
 if = { limit = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } add = { value = market_goods_pricier multiply = 20 } }
 if = { limit = { market_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = { value = market_goods_cheaper multiply = 20 } }
 } }
 min = 0
 max = 40
}
sed_investment_observed_price_wood_lower = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sg:wood ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } }
  add = 15
  sg:wood ?= {
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.1 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.1 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.2 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.2 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.3 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.3 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.4 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.4 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.5 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.6 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.6 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.7 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.7 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.75 } add = 1 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.75 } subtract = 1 }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { sg:wood ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } } } add = owner.var:sed_investment_market_wood_lower }
 min = 0
 max = 40
}
sed_investment_price_wood_lower = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_wood_lower } add = var:sed_investment_local_wood_lower }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_wood_lower } } add = sed_investment_observed_price_wood_lower }
}

 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_electricity_upper } add = var:sed_investment_local_electricity_upper }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_electricity_upper } } add = sed_investment_observed_price_electricity_upper }
}
sed_investment_market_price_iron_lower = {
 value = 30
 market ?= { mg:iron ?= {
 if = { limit = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } add = { value = market_goods_pricier multiply = 40 } }
 if = { limit = { market_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = { value = market_goods_cheaper multiply = 40 } }
 } }
 min = 0
 max = 80
}
sed_investment_observed_price_iron_lower = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sg:iron ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } }
  add = 30
  sg:iron ?= {
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.1 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.1 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.2 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.2 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.3 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.3 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.4 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.4 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.5 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.6 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.6 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.7 } add = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.7 } subtract = 4 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.75 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.75 } subtract = 2 }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { sg:iron ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } } } add = owner.var:sed_investment_market_iron_lower }
 min = 0
 max = 80
}

sed_investment_logging_camp_output_hardwood = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_hardwood } } add = 10 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_increased_hardwood } } add = 20 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_simple_forestry } } add = 30 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_saw_mills } } add = 60 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_electric_saw_mills } } add = 100 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_hardwood } } add = -25 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_increased_hardwood } } add = -40 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }

  add = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_hardwood_lower }
  add = { value = 30 multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }

  add = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_hardwood multiply = sed_investment_price_hardwood_lower }
  add = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_electricity multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_engines multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_transportation multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } } add = 30 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp } add = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_return = {
 value = sed_investment_logging_camp_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp var:sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_logging_camp }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_logging_camp }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp var:sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_return_national = {
 value = sed_investment_logging_camp_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
}
sed_investment_wood_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_wood_logging_camp_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_logging_camp_margin }
}
sed_investment_wood_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_wood_logging_camp_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_logging_camp_output }
}
sed_investment_wood_return = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_wood_logging_camp_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_logging_camp_return }
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_output_iron = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_picks_and_shovels_building_iron_mine } } add = 20 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_atmospheric_engine_pump_building_iron_mine } } add = 40 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_condensing_engine_pump_building_iron_mine } } add = 60 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_diesel_pump_building_iron_mine } } add = 70 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_nitroglycerin_building_iron_mine } } add = 12 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_iron_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_dynamite_building_iron_mine } } add = 20 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }

  add = { value = 20 multiply = sed_investment_price_iron_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 5 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }

  add = { value = sed_investment_iron_mine_output_iron multiply = sed_investment_price_iron_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_engines multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_explosives multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_transportation multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } } add = 20 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine } add = sed_investment_iron_mine_output_iron }
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_return = {
 value = sed_investment_iron_mine_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine var:sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_iron_mine }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_iron_mine_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_iron_mine }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine var:sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_iron_mine_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}

 if = { limit = { b:building_munition_plant = { has_active_production_method = pm_explosive_shells } } add = 90 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_munition_plant_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }

  add = { value = 50 multiply = sed_investment_price_ammunition_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 5 multiply = sed_investment_price_copper_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = 20 multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = 15 multiply = sed_investment_price_lead_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }

  add = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_output_ammunition multiply = sed_investment_price_ammunition_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_copper multiply = sed_investment_price_copper_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_electricity multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_explosives multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_lead multiply = sed_investment_price_lead_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_munition_plant_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_munition_plant_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } } add = 50 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant } add = sed_investment_munition_plant_output_ammunition }
}
sed_investment_munition_plant_return = {
 value = sed_investment_munition_plant_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant var:sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_munition_plant }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_munition_plant }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant var:sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}

   subtract = { value = sed_investment_chemical_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}

 value = sed_investment_chemical_plant_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_chemical_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_chemical_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_chemical_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }

 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_picks_and_shovels_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 20 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_atmospheric_engine_pump_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 40 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_condensing_engine_pump_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 60 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_diesel_pump_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 80 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_nitroglycerin_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 10 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_sulfur_mine = { has_active_production_method = pm_dynamite_building_sulfur_mine } } add = 20 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_sulfur_mine_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine } }

  add = { value = 20 multiply = sed_investment_price_sulfur_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 5 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine }

  add = { value = sed_investment_sulfur_mine_output_sulfur multiply = sed_investment_price_sulfur_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_engines multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_explosives multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_sulfur_mine_transportation multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_sulfur_mine_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine } } add = 20 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine } add = sed_investment_sulfur_mine_output_sulfur }
}

  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_sulfur_mine }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_sulfur_mine_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_sulfur_mine }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_sulfur_mine var:sed_investment_wage_known_sulfur_mine = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_sulfur_mine_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }

sed_growth_wood_demand = { value = 0
 owner = { market = { mg:wood ?= { add = market_goods_buy_orders } } }
}
sed_growth_wood_score = { value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_growth_wood_candidate = yes }
  add = { value = sed_investment_wood_return divide = 200 multiply = 100 }
  # Diversifying into the first national producer breaks ties toward new industry.
  if = { limit = { sed_wood_found_site = yes }
   add = { value = sed_investment_wood_return divide = 200 multiply = 50 }
  }
  # The factor is based on a mature building average, not marginal causality.
  multiply = sed_perf_wood_factor
  multiply = sed_strategy_wood_factor
  add = { value = sed_site_readiness multiply = 0.000001 }
 }
}

  has_variable = sed_perf_wood_expected var:sed_perf_wood_expected > 0
  has_variable = sed_perf_wood_actual
  var:sed_perf_wood_actual < { value = var:sed_perf_wood_expected multiply = 0.5 }
 } subtract = 0.5 }
}

sed_strategy_wood_factor = { value = 1
 if = { limit = { owner = { total_population <= 1000000 } } add = 0.2 }


 if = { limit = { OR = { AND = { sed_wood_logging_camp_investment_eligible = yes sed_strategy_logging_camp_domestic_inputs = yes } } } add = 0.2 }
 # Déficit de oferta do mercado não é uma medida de importação do país.
 add = { value = 0 owner = { market = { mg:wood ?= {
  if = { limit = { market_goods_buy_orders > 0 }
   add = { value = market_goods_buy_orders subtract = market_goods_sell_orders
    divide = market_goods_buy_orders min = 0 max = 1 multiply = 0.2 }
  }
 } } } }
 min = 1 max = 1.75
}
